Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the block framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 29. The resulting operator achieves bound O(109/n^2).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to block theory, we decompose the problem into 29 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 109. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on block structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 859. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 499 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by block generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 859, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 499. QED.
